```
164:def exec (P : Program) (dbase : Name → Option Word) (pad : Name → Nat)

/-- Clocked big-step semantics with outcomes. Ctrl's equations verbatim for the
    shared constructs; every recursive call is at `fuel` from `fuel+1`.

    `call`: lookup + arity check (`wf` guarantees them; dynamic `none` keeps the
    semantics total), enter the frame, run the body accepting `normal`/`ret`
    (an escaping brk/cont is `none` — `wf` bans it), then return to the CALLER's
    registers and `sp` (structural restore), with only `rets` copied back and
    the callee's memory kept. -/
def exec (P : Program) (dbase : Name → Option Word) (pad : Name → Nat)
    (stackLo : Word) :
    Nat → Stmt → St → Option (St × Outcome)
  | 0,      _,    _ => none
  | fuel+1, stmt, s =>
    match stmt with
    | .skip            => some (s, .normal)
    | .annot _         => some (s, .normal)
    | .cref rd d       =>
        match dbase d with
        | some a => some (s.rset rd a, .normal)
        | none   => none
    | .clen rd d       =>
        match List.lookup d P.data with
        | some bs => some (s.rset rd (BitVec.ofNat 64 bs.length), .normal)
        | none    => none
    | .addi rd rs imm  => some (s.rset rd (s.rget rs + imm.signExtend 64), .normal)
    | .add  rd r1 r2   => some (s.rset rd (s.rget r1 + s.rget r2), .normal)
    | .sub  rd r1 r2   => some (s.rset rd (s.rget r1 - s.rget r2), .normal)
    | .orr  rd r1 r2   => some (s.rset rd (s.rget r1 ||| s.rget r2), .normal)
    | .slli rd rs sh   => some (s.rset rd (s.rget rs <<< sh), .normal)
    | .srli rd rs sh   => some (s.rset rd (s.rget rs >>> sh), .normal)
    | .lbu  rd rs imm  => let a := s.rget rs + imm.signExtend 64
                          some (s.rset rd ((s.loadByte a).setWidth 64), .normal)
    | .sb   rb rv imm  => let a := s.rget rb + imm.signExtend 64
                          some (s.storeByte a ((s.rget rv).setWidth 8), .normal)
    | .ld   rd rs imm  => let a := s.rget rs + imm.signExtend 64
                          some (s.rset rd (s.loadWord a), .normal)
    | .sd   rb rv imm  => let a := s.rget rb + imm.signExtend 64
                          some (s.storeWord a (s.rget rv), .normal)
    | .ife c a b t e   =>
        if evalCond c (s.rget a) (s.rget b)
        then exec P dbase pad stackLo fuel t s else exec P dbase pad stackLo fuel e s
    | .seq a b         =>
        match exec P dbase pad stackLo fuel a s with
        | some (s', .normal) => exec P dbase pad stackLo fuel b s'
        | other              => other
    | .block body      =>
        match exec P dbase pad stackLo fuel body s with
        | some (s', .normal)     => some (s', .normal)
        | some (s', .brk 0)      => some (s', .normal)
        | some (s', .brk (k+1))  => some (s', .brk k)
        | some (s', .cont k)     => some (s', .cont k)
        | some (s', .ret)        => some (s', .ret)
        | none                   => none
    | .while c a b body =>
        if evalCond c (s.rget a) (s.rget b) then
          match exec P dbase pad stackLo fuel body s with
          | some (s', .normal)     => exec P dbase pad stackLo fuel (.while c a b body) s'
          | some (s', .cont 0)     => exec P dbase pad stackLo fuel (.while c a b body) s'
          | some (s', .cont (k+1)) => some (s', .cont k)
          | some (s', .brk k)      => some (s', .brk k)
          | some (s', .ret)        => some (s', .ret)
          | none                   => none
        else some (s, .normal)
    | .brkB k          => some (s, .brk k)
    | .contL k         => some (s, .cont k)
    | .ret             => some (s, .ret)
    | .call argc rvc f args rets =>
        match List.lookup f P.env with
        | none => none
        | some fd =>
          if fd.argc == argc && fd.rvc == rvc then
            match frameEnter stackLo fd (pad f) (args.toList.map s.rget) s.mem s.sp with
            | none => none                                       -- stack overflow
            | some callee =>
              match exec P d

… [truncated 537 chars]
```

LowIR/Compile.lean:148:def maxRegS : PStmt → Nat
def maxRegS : PStmt → Nat
  | .skip | .annot _ | .ret | .brkB _ | .contL _ => 0
  | .seq a b          => max (maxRegS a) (maxRegS b)
  | .addi rd rs _     => max rd rs
  | .add  rd r1 r2    => max rd (max r1 r2)
  | .sub  rd r1 r2    => max rd (max r1 r2)
  | .orr  rd r1 r2    => max rd (max r1 r2)
  | .slli rd rs _     => max rd rs
  | .srli rd rs _     => max rd rs
  | .lbu  rd rs _     => max rd rs
  | .ld   rd rs _     => max rd rs
  | .sb   rb rv _     => max rb rv
  | .sd   rb rv _     => max rb rv
  | .ife _ a b t e    => max (max a b) (max (maxRegS t) (maxRegS e))
  | .while _ a b body => max (max a b) (maxRegS body)
  | .block body       => maxRegS body
  | .call _ _ _ args rets =>
      max (args.toList.foldl max 0) (rets.toList.foldl max 0)
  | .cref rd _        => rd
  | .clen rd _        => rd
```

def MemAccOff (L : Layout) (holes : List Hole)
    (P : Program) (dbase : Name → Option Word) (pad : Name → Nat) (stackLo : Word) :
    Nat → PStmt → St → Prop
  | _, .ld _ rs imm, s =>
      ∀ i, i < 8 → ¬ MachPriv L holes (s.rget rs + imm.signExtend 64 + BitVec.ofNat 64 i)
  | _, .lbu _ rs imm, s => ¬ MachPriv L holes (s.rget rs + imm.signExtend 64)
  | _, .sd rb _ imm, s =>
      (s.rget rb + imm.signExtend 64).toNat + 8 ≤ 2 ^ 64 ∧
        ∀ i, i < 8 → ¬ MachPriv L holes (s.rget rb + imm.signExtend 64 + BitVec.ofNat 64 i)
  | _, .sb rb _ imm, s => ¬ MachPriv L holes (s.rget rb + imm.signExtend 64)
  | fuel + 1, .seq a b, s =>
      MemAccOff L holes P dbase pad stackLo fuel a s ∧
        (∀ s1, LowIR.Prog.exec P dbase pad stackLo fuel a s = some (s1, .normal) →
           MemAccOff L holes P dbase pad stackLo fuel b s1)
  | _, _, _ => True
```

### 🤖 **Assistant**  ·  20:58:30; model 14.5s

The outcome-carrying `lower_sim` and the `block` case are done and committed (`aab5b77`).

## `lower_sim_cf` — the outcome-carrying simulation

This is the generalization the compound control-flow ops were blocked on. Where the old `lower_sim` is `.normal`-only and always lands at the fall-through, this one:

- **Carries the `Outcome`** and lands the machine at `codeBase + landPos(outcome)` — fall-through for `.normal`, the resolved break/continue/epilogue label for `.brk`/`.cont`/`.ret`.
- **Inducts over `emitCF`** (the validated label-aware emit) with the label-position environment `brkPos`/`contPos`/`epiPos`.
- **Threads `LabelsOk` + a `here + 4·csize < 2²⁰` bound** so every jump offset fits the 21-bit window (both ends `< 2²⁰` ⟹ offset in `(−2²⁰, 2²⁰)`).

## The `block` case

The key insight that makes it clean: **`block` emits no machine code of its own** — the `lEnd` label is 0 bytes — so its machine trace *is* the body's trace. The whole proof is:

1. Apply the IH to `body` with `lEnd = here + 4·csize body` pushed onto the break stack.
2. Case-walk on the *body's* outcome, showing the landing positions coincide with the *block's* result:
   - `.brk 0` (the catch) → body lands at `lEnd`, which *is* the block's fall-through — so it becomes `.normal` landing at the right place, no code moved.
   - `.brk (k+1)` / `.cont k` / `.ret` propagate one frame out — pure `getD_cons_succ` bookkeeping (`(lEnd :: brkPos).getD (k+1) = brkPos.getD k`).

`skip`/`annot` are also done; the remaining cases are `all_goals sorry`.

Two things worth noting from the build:
- I had to add a `.block` case to `MemAccOff` (= the body's) so the memory-access side-condition threads through — and hit the **stale-olean gotcha**: `lake env lean` reads compiled imports, so my edit reduced to the `True` catch-all until I rebuilt the lib. The `simp only [MemAccOff]` reducing `haccess` to `True` is exactly the symptom that flagged it.
- `MemAccOff`/`emitCF`/`maxRegS` on `.block body` are defeq to the body forms but Lean won't unfold them at an application site, so they're rebound with explicit `have`s.

**Next**, in rough order of ease: the leaf jumps (`ret`/`brkB`/`contL` — the `*_sim` atoms already exist, just need the range plumbing from `LabelsOk`/`hbnd`), then `seq` (outcome case-split on the first half), then `ife`/`while` (branch selection + back-edge, all atoms in place). Want me to continue with the leaf jumps and `seq` next?

### 🤖 **Assistant**  ·  21:04:02; model 13.8s

Committed (`d5d6a96`). The leaf jumps and `seq` are done.

## `ret` / `brkB` / `contL`

Each is a one-liner over `jump_sim`: the machine jumps to the resolved target, which is *exactly* `landPos` of that outcome — `epiPos` for `.ret`, `brkPos.getD k 0` for `.brk k`, `contPos.getD k 0` for `.cont k`. The IL state is unchanged (`s' = s`, `k = 1`). The only real work is the range obligation — the offset `target − here` must fit the 21-bit `jal` window — discharged from `LabelsOk` (all label positions `< 2²⁰`) plus the `here + 4·csize < 2²⁰` bound, with `getD_lt` keeping `brkB`/`contL`'s target bounded (the `getD` default `0` is bounded too, so an out-of-range index is fine).

## `seq`

Invert `exec (.seq a b)` on `a`'s outcome:

- **`a` is `.normal`** → chain the two IHs. `a` lands at its fall-through, which is exactly `b`'s start position; `StInv_sp_eq` transfers the frame side-conditions to the intermediate IL state `s1`; `Emitted_append` + `emitCF_length` places `b`'s code at `here + 4·csize a`; `stepN_add` composes the two step-counts. The seq's outcome is `b`'s.
- **`a` is `.brk`/`.cont`/`.ret`** → it short-circuits (`b` never runs). The IH on `a` *already* lands the machine at the external target, and — the key observation — `landPos` ignores the fall-through for non-normal outcomes, so the seq's landing coincides with `a`'s directly. No chaining needed.

That non-normal short-circuit falling out for free is the payoff of the `landPos` design: the same landing function that makes `block` work makes `seq`'s early exits work.

`lower_sim_cf` now covers **skip / annot / block / ret / brkB / contL / seq** — the whole control-flow *scaffolding* plus the leaves. What's left under `all_goals sorry`: the 6 arith + 4 memory ops (which should delegate to the already-proven `lower_sim`), then `ife`/`while` (branch-selection + back-edge, atoms all in place), then `cref`/`clen`/`call`. Want me to knock out the arith/mem delegation next (likely quick), or go straight for `ife`/`while`?

### 🤖 **Assistant**  ·  21:10:14; model 14.0s

Committed (`c68a8f6`). The arith/mem delegation is done — 10 near-identical one-shots.

## The delegation pattern

Every arith op (`addi`/`add`/`sub`/`orr`/`slli`/`srli`) and memory op (`ld`/`sd`/`lbu`/`sb`) is a straight-line statement: `.normal` outcome, `emitCF = emit` (the catch-all), and `csize = (emit _).length`. So in `lower_sim_cf` each one just reduces to the already-proven `lower_sim`:

1. `rw [exec_OP] … at hexec; obtain ⟨rfl, rfl⟩` — pins `oc = .normal` and `s'` to the op's canonical result.
2. `lower_sim (fuel+1) (.OP …) …` — the `.normal` simulation, taking the same `StInv`/`Emitted`/frame hypotheses (`emitCF (.OP) = emit (.OP)` and `MemAccOff` line up definitionally, so `hem`/`haccess` pass straight through).
3. `by rw [hpck]; simp only [landPos, csize]` — reconcile the landing: `landPos .normal = fall-through = here + 4·csize = here + 4·|emit|`.

The nice part is how cleanly the two-layer design pays off: the *semantic* work (that these ops preserve `StInv` and advance the pc correctly) was already done once in `lower_sim`; `lower_sim_cf` doesn't re-prove any of it, it just wraps each in the outcome-carrying frame and checks the fall-through equals the generic landing. No new axioms, no new arithmetic.

## Where `lower_sim_cf` stands

It now covers the **entire straight-line + memory + control-flow-scaffold fragment**:

| Done | Remaining (`all_goals sorry`) |
|------|------|
| skip, annot | ife, while |
| 6 arith, 4 mem | cref, clen |
| seq, block | call |
| ret, brkB, contL | |

The remaining five are the substantive ones: `ife`/`while` need the branch-selection (`step_beq`/`evalCond` picking then/else or body/exit) and the `while` back-edge — but all those atoms exist. `cref`/`clen` pull in the data-segment addressing, and `call` is the frame-enter/exit. `ife`/`while` are the natural next target since they complete the control-flow story and reuse `jump_sim` + the branch steps. Want me to take on `ife` next?

No new security-relevant constraints stated this session.

2. Key Technical Concepts:
   - **Axiom hygiene**: target `[propext, Quot.sound]`. Avoid `omega` on compound goals (∧/↔/∨ → Classical.choice), `by_cases`, `native_decide`/`decide` on big structures (`ofReduceBool`), stdlib `signExtend` lemmas. Use `cases Nat.decLe`, constructive Or walks, `rw [Int.bmod_def]; split <;> omega` for the bmod identity at concrete modulus.
   - **StInv relation** (6 conjuncts, P1 makes it plain equality): `m.rget 2 = s.sp`; slot invariant; `Installed L m`; off-MachPriv memory agreement; `holes.head? = some(s.sp, userOff fd)`; sp%8=0.
   - **Compiler lowering**: `loadSlot r t` = `if r=0 [addi t 0 0] else [ld t SP (slotOff r)]` (always 1 instr); `storeSlot r t` = `if r=0 [] else [sd SP t (slotOff r)]` (0 or 1). `symSize`: label=0, cref=20, else 4. Control flow uses SymInstr (.label/.br/.jmp), positioned by `layoutItems` (returns `List (Nat×Nat)` label table), resolved by `resolveOne` (jmp→jal0(tgt-pos), br→condInstr(tgt-pos)).
   - **condInstr c a b off** = `.beq/.blt/.bge/.bgeu a b (BitVec.ofInt 13 off)` for c=.eq/.lt/.ge/.geu. **jal0 off** = `.jal 0 (BitVec.ofInt 21 off)`. T0=5, T1=6, SP=2.
   - **evalCond** (returns Bool): `.eq => x = y`, `.lt => x.slt y`, `.ge => !x.slt y`, `.geu => !x.ult y`.
   - **Machine branch semantics** (step): beq branches (pc+imm.signExtend) if rget rs1 = rget rs2 else next(pc+4); blt if slt; bge if ¬slt else jump; bgeu if ¬ult else jump. jal: `(s.rset rd (pc+4)).setPc (pc + imm.signExtend 64)`.
   - **exec block semantics**: matches `exec fuel body s`: .normal→.normal, .brk 0→.normal (catch), .brk(k+1)→.brk k, .cont k→.cont k, .ret→.ret, none→none.
   - **exec ife**: `if evalCond c (s.rget a)(s.rget b) then exec t s else exec e s`.
   - **exec seq**: match exec a: some(s',.normal)→exec b s', other→other.
   - **stepN** (own iteration, f^[n] is Mathlib-only): stepN 0 = m, stepN 1 m = step m (rfl), stepN_add.
   - **@[inline] gotcha**: St.rget/rset/storeByte/loadByte don't unfold via simp[name]; use FULL-name `unfold`. Concrete-index reads hold by rfl.
   - **Stale olean gotcha**: `lake env lean X.lean` uses compiled oleans of imports; edits to imported files (StmtSim) need `lake build` first or reductions silently use old definitions.

3. Files and Code Sections:

     - Added before lower_sim: `jump_sim (L fd holes s m)(here tgt)(δ)(hinv)(hpc: m.pc = codeBase + ofNat here)(hem: Emitted L here [Instr.jal 0 (ofInt 21 δ)])(hδ: δ = tgt-here)(hlo)(hhi) : StInv s (step m) ∧ (step m).pc = codeBase + ofNat tgt`. Proof: decode_at with `by rw [hpc, Nat.mul_zero, Nat.add_zero]`, step_jal, StInv_scratch (a=0≠2), signExtend_ofInt_21+jump_lands.

131	## 0. Mission and payoff
132	
133	Prove: **if the D7/D8 IL says a program computes something, the compiled RV64I
134	bytes compute the same thing on the trusted `Rv64i` model.** This is the T1
135	of `LowIR.lean:312` re-targeted at the real IR (functions, frames, calls,
136	const data) — the pass that amortizes the flat-PC simulation cost paid
137	per-program in `Hex0/Refine.lean` (~3400 lines for ONE program) into ONE
138	proof reused by every program. After it, a structured-altitude proof like
139	`strlen_correct` transports to actual bytes by composition, and the ProgLib
140	functions get machine-level correctness for the price of their (easy)
141	IL-level proofs.

## `ife` case of `lower_sim_cf` — complete

The two-armed conditional, proven end-to-end against the machine `step`, in three commits:

**1. Infrastructure (`82c8452`)**
- `cond_taken` / `cond_not_taken` — bridge the machine branch to the IL condition: a `condInstr c T0 T1 δ` whose registers hold `x,y` is taken iff `evalCond c x y`. Handles the compiler's positive-form encoding where `.ge`/`.geu` take the *else* arm of `bge`/`bgeu` (extract the Prop/Bool fact via `simpa [evalCond]`, resolve the machine's `if` with `if_pos`/`if_neg`).
- `BranchOk` — the 13-bit branch-span side condition (`8 + 4·csize e < 2¹²`, exactly `resolveOne`'s `δ ≤ 4094` guard). Threaded through the statement + the block/seq IH calls; discharged per-program by the real layout.

**2. The proof (`a798d80`)**
- Two `run_load`s bring the operands into `T0,T1` (pc → `here+8`);
- decode the branch, then `cases evalCond`:
  - **true** → branch taken to `lT = here+16+4·csize e` (via `signExtend_ofInt_13` + `jump_lands`), IH on the then-arm (uniform);

- Also added an `ife` case to `MemAccOff` (both arms must be access-safe).

Key gotchas recorded in memory: `stepN_add` is `stepN(a+b) m = stepN b (stepN a m)` and `stepN` steps-first, so `stepN(K+1) m = step(stepN K m)` needs `rw [stepN_add]; rfl`; `jump_sim`'s `hδ` needs `push_cast; omega` for literal offsets.
